- **activation-reviewer**: Pre-activation / pre-completion substance review of an Issue (dir-mode artifact, SPEC §1.7 / §2.1 / §4.9). Type-neutral — dispatches on the Issue's type label. For Directives: called by `/file-directive` (proposed body), `/activate` (re-check before promoting), `/revise-directive` (revised body), and `/complete-directive` (completion claim). For Execution Issues (`task`/`execution`/`bug`): called by `/activate` at activation time. For Initiatives: called by `/consume-initiative` (contract-evaluability + extraction-faithfulness). - **acto-fixer**: Applies the MINIMAL fix for exactly ONE pinned divergence found by acto-critic. The failing test pins the divergence; the fix makes that test pass. Never bundles multiple fixes. Never refactors adjacent code. Single-file scope (escalate to acto-builder if the fix spans files). After the fix, runs the full gauntlet and reports honestly whether it passes. Dispatch one acto-fixer per blocker issue, serially. Always followed by an acto-critic re-audit. ([Corvidae-Coding-Projects/Thermite](https://github.com/Corvidae-Coding-Projects/Thermite/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/acto-fixer.md) · ★54)

- **adversary**: Tries to break things, under one evidence standard: a finding is executable or it is not a finding. Two modes. REFUTE — given a change or a design, find the input, ordering, crash point, concurrent caller, or false premise that makes it wrong. PROOF — given a claim ("this bug exists", "this fix works"), reproduce the bug on today's code and prove the fix eliminates it. Mandatory as the second pass on Tier-3 changes, on any design before it is built, and on any scoring, ranking, abstention, or presentation change. ([scrypster/muninndb](https://github.com/scrypster/muninndb/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/adversary.md) · ★331)
- **adversary**: Pre-implementation red-team analysis. Challenges plans BEFORE code is written — edge cases, security holes, scalability bottlenecks, error propagation. Catches flaws at plan time (10x cheaper). ([Rune-kit/rune](https://github.com/Rune-kit/rune/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★87 · 2 copies)
- **adversary**: QA - assume broken, find edge cases, prove with evidence ([ariaxhan/kernel-claude](https://github.com/ariaxhan/kernel-claude/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★13)
- **adversary**: Finds every weakness, flaw, and failure mode in an idea. Authentic dissenter, not assigned devil's advocacy. Use proactively when stress-testing, red teaming, poking holes, or when the user asks what could go wrong, what they're missing, or to challenge something. ([damionrashford/Adversaria](https://github.com/damionrashford/Adversaria/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★7)
- **adversary**: Adversarially attacks any artifact (spec, architecture, plan, diff/PR, README, or prose claim) — assumes it is wrong, broken, or incomplete and tries to prove it, then reports only the findings that survive its own refutation attempts. Use as a skeptical-by-default lens; dispatched by /adversary and available to any skill or workflow that wants an adversarial pass. Read-only. ([highflame-ai/ai-factory](https://github.com/highflame-ai/ai-factory/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★5)
- **adversary**: Hyper-critical code reviewer for VDD workflow ([rand/disciplined-process-plugin](https://github.com/rand/disciplined-process-plugin/blob/HEAD/disciplined-process-plugin/agents/adversary.md) · ★2)
- **adversary**: Independent adversarial code evaluator — finds logic holes, edge cases, and quality issues in a fresh context ([2233admin/adversarial-harness](https://github.com/2233admin/adversarial-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: The Adversary. Judges work it did not write. Never repairs. Approves when residual risks are recorded. ([attac-t/the-foundry](https://github.com/attac-t/the-foundry/blob/HEAD/plugins/panel/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: 高精確度開發模式的攻擊者（紅隊），假設實作有漏洞，嘗試找出讓它失敗的輸入 ([chinlung/claude-dev-workflow](https://github.com/chinlung/claude-dev-workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/high-precision-dev/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Read-only design adversary (devil's-advocate) for the design-buddy debate. Given a proposed design, the recon dossier, the design-buddy principles, and the host repo's stated hard rules, it attacks the proposal — architectural flaws, data/safety gaps, simpler alternatives — citing every DF-*/DN-* principle breached by ID and every host hard rule by verbatim quote + path:line. Returns structured objections; never writes, never coordinates with the proposer. ([davcs86/agent-plugins](https://github.com/davcs86/agent-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/design-buddy/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Adversarial design reviewer. Reviews a plan or a changeset at architecture altitude — structural soundness, maintainability, alternatives — and is deliberately skeptical to counter an author's self-approval bias. Explicitly does NOT do style, formatting, nits, or minor/local bugs (linters, type-checkers, /code-review, and caveman:cavecrew-reviewer own those). Read-only: it never modifies code. Spawned by /jador:critique. ([Jador/skills](https://github.com/Jador/skills/blob/HEAD/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: The third and final v1 agent in `spp`, and the only one that is **opt-in**. The adversary is enabled by setting `ADVERSARY_FLAG = on` in `plan.md` §8 and the corresponding field in `loop_spec.md` §4; off by default. ([JayLBean/supervised-prompt-producer](https://github.com/JayLBean/supervised-prompt-producer/blob/HEAD/skills/run/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Generates 2-3 additional debugging hypotheses that attack gaps in primary list — especially config, env, deployment, human error, upstream/downstream, invalid-premise failures. ([soummyaanon/god-of-debugger](https://github.com/soummyaanon/god-of-debugger/blob/HEAD/plugins/god-of-debugger/agents/adversary.md) · ★1)
- **adversary**: Adversarial diff reviewer for ultracode workflows — a kill-mandate reviewer that assumes the code is wrong and must earn any pass verdict. Read-only; returns severity-sorted, evidence-gated findings and a BLUF verdict, never edits code. Spawn 2+ per implementer with distinct lenses (correctness, security, gaming, cross-file, spec) via agentType 'adversary'. Also useful solo for a hostile second opinion on any diff or spec. - **aesop-bridge-implementer**: Executes exactly one task brief from .claude/kits/aesop-bridge/TASKS.md against the polytropos plugin. Dispatch one task per invocation during /polytropos:execute aesop-bridge, passing the task's model field as the Agent tool's model parameter. ([agentmc15/polytropos](https://github.com/agentmc15/polytropos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aesop-bridge-implementer.md))
- **aesop-bridge-reviewer**: Phase-boundary review of the aesop-bridge kit. - **aesop-bridge-verifier**: Fresh-context adversarial verification of a single completed aesop-bridge task. Dispatch after the implementer reports success, with just the task id. ([agentmc15/polytropos](https://github.com/agentmc15/polytropos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aesop-bridge-verifier.md))
- **aesop-fold-implementer**: Executes exactly one task brief from .claude/kits/aesop-fold/TASKS.md against the polytropos repo. - **aesop-fold-reviewer**: Reviews each completed aesop-fold phase against PLAN.md before the next phase starts. Dispatch at phase boundaries during /polytropos:execute aesop-fold with the phase number. - **aesop-fold-test-author**: Dispatch aesop-fold-test-author during /polytropos:execute aesop-fold after the implementer reports a task done and before the verifier runs, for tasks whose kit declared the `test-author` role. ([agentmc15/polytropos](https://github.com/agentmc15/polytropos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/aesop-fold-test-author.md))
- **aesop-fold-verifier**: Fresh-context adversarial verification of a single completed aesop-fold task. Dispatch after the implementer reports success, with just the task id.
